CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[AddGdata]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
@_machineNumber int,
@_enrollNumber int,
@_enrollMachineNumber int,
@_verifyMode int,
@_inOutMode int,
@_year int,
@_month int,
@_day int,
@_hour int,
@_minute int,
@takeJoureny int,
@_statu bit

AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

   insert into Attendance(_machineNumber,_enrollNumber,
_enrollMachineNumber,_verifyMode,_inOutMode,_year,_month,_day,_hour,_minute,takeJoureny,_statu)values(@_machineNumber,@_enrollNumber,@_enrollMachineNumber,@_verifyMode,@_inOutMode,@_year,@_month,@_day,@_hour,@_minute,@takeJoureny,@_statu)

END
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[CalcItemStock]    Script Date: 5/25/2021 1:42:26 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CalcItemStock]
	@branch [int],
	@Inventory [int],
	@item [int],
	@stock Float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
 set @stock = (select  sum(stockIn - stockOut) as ItemStock from (
select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item and inv_sub.store=@Inventory and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store) as tb  )
	if (@stock is null) set @stock=0
END
GO

/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[TotalItemStockInventory]    Script Date: 6/15/2021 4:30:57 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE function [dbo].[TotalItemStockInventory](@branch int,@date datetime ,@Inventory int=1)  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock,Category,Inventory   from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,inv_sub.store as Inventory ,ItemsCategory.id as Category  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and Inv.date&lt;=@date  and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id 
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut ,inv_sub.store as Inventory ,ItemsCategory.id as Category from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and Inv.date&lt;=@date and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id) as tb group by ItemId,Inventory,Category)

GO

CREATE function [dbo].[TotalItemStock](@branch int,@date datetime )  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock,Category from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut ,ItemsCategory.id as Category  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store is not null and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and Inv.date&lt;=@date  and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id 
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut,ItemsCategory.id as Category from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store is not null and (@branch is null or inv.branch=@branch) and Inv.date&lt;=@date and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id) as tb group by ItemId,Category)
GO

CREATE function [dbo].[Inventorybalance]()  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock,Inventory from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,inv_sub.store as Inventory   from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store is not null and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id 
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut ,inv_sub.store as Inventory  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store  is not null and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id) as tb group by ItemId,Inventory)
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[SalesByDay]    Script Date: 10/22/2024 12:57:59 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE function [dbo].[SalesByDay](@branch int,@InvType int,@StartDate  dateTime,@EndDate  dateTime )  
returns table  
as
return(select InvDate ,sum(SaleTotal - ReturnSaleTotal) as Total,sum(SaleTax - ReturnSaleTax) as TotalTax,  sum(InvTotal-ReturnTotal) as SaleNet,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY InvDate DESC) AS RowNum from
(select  CAST(date AS DATE) as InvDate, sum(tot_net) as InvTotal, sum(inv.InvTotal) as SaleTotal, sum(tax) as SaleTax ,
0 as InvReturnCount,0 as ReturnTotal,0 as  ReturnSaleTotal,0 as ReturnSaleTax
from inv where (@branch is null or  branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=@InvType) and proc_type=1 and IS_Deleted=0 and pay_type &lt;&gt;5 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)  group by date
union all
select  CAST(date AS DATE) as InvDate ,0 as InvCount,0 as InvTotal,0 as SaleTotal,0 as SaleTax, 
sum(tot_net) as ReturnTotal , sum(inv.InvTotal) as ReturnSaleTotal,sum(tax) as ReturnSaleTax
from inv where ( @branch is null or  branch =@branch  ) and (inv_type=@InvType) and proc_type=2 and IS_Deleted=0 and pay_type &lt;&gt;5 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)  group by date  )  as tb group by tb.InvDate )
GO


/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[InventoryCostByType]    Script Date: 6/16/2021 2:19:00 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

create PROCEDURE [dbo].[InventoryCostByType]
	@branch [int],
	@InvType [int],
	@ProcType [int],
	@StockCost float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	 set @StockCost = (select sum(val1*exchange_price) from inv,inv_sub where branch=@branch and inv.inv_type=@InvType and inv.proc_type=@ProcType and inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId and  inv.IS_Deleted=0)
 	if (@StockCost is null) set @StockCost=0
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[InventoryCost]    Script Date: 6/19/2021 3:15:31 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[InventoryCost]
	@branch [int],
	@date datetime,
	@AvrgCost float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	set @AvrgCost = (select  sum(ItemAvrgCost * Stock) as AvrgCost from (
select itemId,sum(ItemAvrgCost) as ItemAvrgCost,sum(Stock)as Stock from(
select  ItemId as ItemId,((sum (ItemAvrgCostIn) - sum(ItemAvrgCostOut))+ sum(ItemDiscountIn) -sum(ItemDiscountOut))/NULLIF(sum(stockIn - stockOut), 0) as ItemAvrgCost,0 as Stock from (
select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut ,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostIn ,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch  and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val)  as stockOut ,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut, 0 as ItemAvrgCostIn ,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8)  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch  and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
 union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut ,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2 and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8)   and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
 union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,0 as ItemDiscountIn ,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountOut,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8)   and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
  ) as tb group by ItemId
union select  itemId,0 as ItemAvrgCost, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut   from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch   and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store 
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch  and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store) as tb group by ItemId)as tb group by ItemId)as tb where Stock&gt;0  )
 	if (@AvrgCost is null) set @AvrgCost=0
	if (@AvrgCost &lt;0) set @AvrgCost=0
END
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[AccountTotalBalance]    Script Date: 6/19/2021 3:17:43 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE function [dbo].[AccountTotalBalance](@branch int=1,@date datetime,@AccType int =1)  
returns table  
as
return(
select Entry_sub.acc_no as AccountCode,Accounts_Index.AName as AccountName,sum(Entry_sub.dept) as Debet ,sum(Entry_sub.credit) as Credit from Entry,Entry_sub,Accounts_Index where Entry_sub.branch=@branch  and Entry.IS_Deleted=0 and Entry.state=1 and Entry.date&lt;=@date and Entry.GlobalId=Entry_sub.EntryGlobalId and Entry_sub.acc_no=Accounts_Index.Code and Accounts_Index.FinalAcc=@AccType
 group by Entry_sub.acc_no,Accounts_Index.AName)
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[AccountsTotalBalance]    Script Date: 6/19/2021 3:17:37 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE function [dbo].[AccountsTotalBalance](@branch int=1,@date datetime)  
returns table  
as
return(
 select sum(Debet) as Debet ,sum(Credit) as Credit from (select sum(Entry_sub.dept) as Debet ,sum(Entry_sub.credit) as Credit from Entry,Entry_sub,Accounts_Index where Entry_sub.branch=@branch  and Entry.IS_Deleted=0 and Entry.state=1 and Entry.date&lt;=@date and Entry.GlobalId=Entry_sub.EntryGlobalId and Entry_sub.acc_no=Accounts_Index.Code and Accounts_Index.FinalAcc=1) as tb)
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[ItemBalanceWithAvrgCost]    Script Date: 6/19/2021 3:19:04 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE function [dbo].[ItemBalanceWithAvrgCost]()  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock,(sum (ItemAvrgCostIn) - sum(ItemAvrgCostOut)) as InevertoryBalance from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,sum(IsNull(inv_sub.AvrgCost,inv_sub.exchange_price)*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostIn ,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store is not null and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut ,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,sum(IsNull(inv_sub.AvrgCost,inv_sub.exchange_price)*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store  is not null and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId) as tb group by ItemId)
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[ItemsExpirationStock]    Script Date: 21-May-22 5:22:20 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[ItemsExpirationStock]()
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN(select ItemId,SUM(stockIn-stockOut) as ItemStock,ItemExpire,items.name as ItemName,items.code as ItemCode,Safes.name as StoreName,tb.store as StoreID,DATEDIFF(YEAR, GETDATE(), ItemExpire) as RemaindeYear , DATEDIFF(MONTH, GETDATE(), ItemExpire) as RemaindeMonths,DATEDIFF(DAY, GETDATE(), ItemExpire) as RemaindeDays,items.group_id as GroupID,tb.BranchId from(
   select inv.branch as BranchId,Inv_Sub.store as store, ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut ,CAST(expire_date AS DATE) as ItemExpire from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where  inv_sub.proc_type=1   and inv.IS_Deleted=0 and Items.ItemProperty=8  group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,CAST(expire_date AS DATE) ,inv.branch
union select inv.branch as BranchId,Inv_Sub.store as store, ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut,CAST(expire_date AS DATE) as ItemExpire from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2   and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 and Items.ItemProperty=8 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,CAST(expire_date AS DATE),inv.branch) as tb left join Items on tb.ItemId=items.id left join Safes on Safes.id=tb.store group by ItemExpire,ItemId,items.name,items.code,Safes.name,items.group_id,tb.BranchId,tb.store)
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ItemAvrgCost]
	@branch [int],
	@item [int],
	@store [int],
	@ItemAvrgCost float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
		 set @ItemAvrgCost = (select  ((sum (ItemAvrgCostIn) - sum(ItemAvrgCostOut))+ sum(ItemDiscountIn) -sum(ItemDiscountOut))/NULLIF(sum(stockIn - stockOut), 0) as ItemAvrgCost from (
 select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut ,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostIn ,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item and (@store is null or Inv_Sub.store = @store) and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val)  as stockOut ,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut, 0 as ItemAvrgCostIn ,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8)  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item and (@store is null or Inv_Sub.store = @store) and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut ,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2 and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8) and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item  and (@store is null or Inv_Sub.store = @store) and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,0 as ItemDiscountIn ,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountOut,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and (inv_type=1  or inv_type= 9  or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8) and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item and (@store is null or Inv_Sub.store = @store) and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store) as tb    )
	if (@ItemAvrgCost is null) set @ItemAvrgCost=0
END
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[ItemsStockInventoryPOS]    Script Date: 6/22/2021 3:52:57 PM ******/
CREATE function [dbo].[ItemsStockInventoryPOS](@branch int=1,@Inventory int=1)  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Stock,Inventory,Category   from (select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,inv_sub.store as Inventory ,ItemsCategory.id as Category  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and Items.ShowInPOS=1 and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and inv.branch=@branch and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id 
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut ,inv_sub.store as Inventory ,ItemsCategory.id as Category from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id left join ItemsCategory on items.group_id= ItemsCategory.id where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and inv.branch=@branch and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,ItemsCategory.id) as tb group by ItemId,Inventory,Category)
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[InventoryCostForBalanceSheet]    Script Date: 6/30/2021 1:35:28 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[InventoryCostForBalanceSheet]
	@branch [int],
	@date datetime,
	@AvrgCost float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	set @AvrgCost = ( select  ((sum (ItemAvrgCostIn) - sum(ItemAvrgCostOut))+ sum(ItemDiscountIn) -sum(ItemDiscountOut)) as ItemAvrgCost from (
select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut ,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostIn ,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_type&lt;&gt; 9 and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv.date&lt;=@date and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut,0 as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,sum(inv_sub.exchange_price*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2 and (inv_type=1 or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8) and inv_type&lt;&gt; 9  and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv.date&lt;=@date and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountIn,0 as ItemDiscountOut ,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2 and (inv_type=1    or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8)and inv_type&lt;&gt; 9  and  inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv.date&lt;=@date and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,0 as ItemDiscountIn ,( sum((((val1*exchange_price)-inv_sub.discount)/(NULLIF(InvTotal, 0)+ minus))* minus)+sum(inv_sub.discount)) as ItemDiscountOut,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and (inv_type=1    or inv_type= 4 or inv_type= 8) and inv_type&lt;&gt; 9   and inv_sub.store is not null and inv.branch=@branch and Inv.date&lt;=@date and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store) as tb  )
 	if (@AvrgCost is null) set @AvrgCost=0
	if (@AvrgCost &lt;0) set @AvrgCost=0
END
GO

create function [dbo].[ItemStockLimits](@branch int=1,@Inventory int=1 )  
returns table  
as
return(
select ItemId, sum(stockIn - stockOut) as ItemStock, IsNull(sum(MaxQntyLimt),0) as MaxQntyLimt ,IsNull(sum(MinQntyLimt),0) as MinQntyLimt from (
select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,0 as MaxQntyLimt,0 as MinQntyLimt   from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1  and inv_sub.store=@Inventory and inv.branch=@branch and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut, items.MaxQtyLimit as MaxQntyLimt,Items.limit as MinQntyLimt  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2   and inv_sub.store =@Inventory and inv.branch=@branch and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv_sub.store,MaxQtyLimit ,limit ) as tb  group by ItemId
)
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CalcItemStockAll]
	@item [int],
	@stock int out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN

 set @stock = (select  sum(stockIn - stockOut) as ItemStock from (
select ItemId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut  from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item  and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId
union select ItemId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and Inv_Sub.ItemId=@item  and  inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId) as tb  )
	if (@stock is null) set @stock=0
END
GO

CREATE function [dbo].[ItemsStockByBranch]()  
returns table  
as
return(
select  itemId,BranchId, InventoryId, sum(stockIn - stockOut)as Quantity,(sum (ItemAvrgCostIn) - sum(ItemAvrgCostOut)) as AvrgCost from (
select ItemId,inv.branch as BranchId,inv_sub.store as InventoryId,sum(val) as stockIn, 0 as stockOut,sum(IsNull(inv_sub.AvrgCost,inv_sub.exchange_price)*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostIn ,0 as ItemAvrgCostOut    from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=1 and inv_sub.store is not null   and inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv.Branch,inv_sub.store
union select ItemId,inv.branch as BranchId,inv_sub.store as InventoryId,0 as stockIn, sum(val) as stockOut,0 as ItemAvrgCostIn,sum(IsNull(inv_sub.AvrgCost,inv_sub.exchange_price)*inv_sub.val1) as ItemAvrgCostOut from inv left join  inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalId=inv_sub.InvGlobalId left join Items on inv_sub.ItemId= Items.id  where inv_sub.proc_type=2  and inv_sub.store is not null and   inv.IS_Deleted=0 group by ItemId,inv.Branch,inv_sub.store) as tb group by ItemId,BranchId, InventoryId )
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[GetSalesByCategory]    Script Date: 10/2/2022 2:33:20 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetSalesByCategory]
	@branch  [int] ,
	@inv_type  [int] ,
	@StartDate  dateTime ,
	@EndDate  dateTime 

WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
select TOP 5 ItemsCategory.name as CategoryName,  ItemsCategory.CategoryID, sum(Sales) as TotalSales,sum(ReturnSales) as ReturnSales , sum(Sales-ReturnSales) as SaleNet  from
(select  ItemId ,  sum(val1*exchange_price) as Sales ,0 as ReturnSales
 from inv  left join inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalID =Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID   where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=@inv_type ) and inv.proc_type=1 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by ItemId 
union
 select   ItemId ,  0 as Sales ,sum(val1*exchange_price) as ReturnSales
 from inv  left join inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalID =Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID  where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=@inv_type) and inv.proc_type=2 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by ItemId   ) as tb left join Items on Items.id= tb.ItemId left join ItemsCategory on Items.group_id= ItemsCategory.CategoryId  group by CategoryID, ItemsCategory.name  order by SaleNet desc
 END
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetSalesByItems]
	@branch  [int] ,
	@inv_type  [int] ,
	@StartDate  dateTime ,
	@EndDate  dateTime 

WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
select TOP 5  Name as ItemName, ItemId, sum(Sales) as TotalSales,sum(ReturnSales) as ReturnSales , sum(Sales-ReturnSales) as SaleNet  from
(select  ItemId ,  sum(val*exchange_price) as Sales ,0 as ReturnSales
 from inv  left join inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalID =Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID   where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=@inv_type  ) and inv.proc_type=1 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by ItemId 
union select  ItemId ,  0 as Sales ,sum(val*exchange_price) as ReturnSales
 from inv  left join inv_sub on inv.InvGlobalID =Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID  where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=@inv_type) and inv.proc_type=2 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by ItemId   
 ) as tb left join Items on Items.id= tb.ItemId where ItemId Is Not Null group by ItemId,name  order   by TotalSales DESC 
 END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[GetSalesByOrderType]    Script Date: 10/2/2022 2:34:32 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetSalesByOrderType]
	@branch  [int] ,
	@inv_type  [int] ,
	@StartDate  dateTime ,
	@EndDate  dateTime 

WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
select '' as OrderTypeName, OrderTypeID, sum(Sales) as TotalSales,sum(ReturnSales) as ReturnSales , sum(Sales-ReturnSales) as SaleNet  from
(select OrderType as OrderTypeID , count(*) as InvCount, sum(cash+visa) as Sales ,0 as ReturnSales
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=2 or inv_type=3) and proc_type=1 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by OrderType
union select OrderType as OrderTypeID , 0as InvCount, 0 as Sales ,sum(cash+visa) as ReturnSales
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=2 or inv_type=3) and proc_type=2 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by OrderType   ) as tb group by OrderTypeID
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[GetSalesByPaymentMethods]    Script Date: 10/2/2022 2:35:19 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetSalesByPaymentMethods]
	@branch  [int] ,
	@inv_type  [int] ,
	@StartDate  dateTime ,
	@EndDate  dateTime 

WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
select '' as PayMethod, PayTypeID, sum(Sales) as TotalSales,sum(ReturnSales) as ReturnSales , sum(Sales-ReturnSales) as SaleNet  from
(select pay_type as PayTypeID , count(*) as InvCount, CASE 
WHEN  pay_type = 1  THEN  sum(tot_net) 
WHEN  pay_type = 2 THEN   sum(visa) 
WHEN  pay_type = 4 THEN   sum(visa + cash)
WHEN  pay_type = -1 THEN  sum(tot_net) 
End as Sales ,0 as ReturnSales
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=2 or inv_type=3) and proc_type=1 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by pay_type
union select pay_type as PayTypeID , 0as InvCount, 0 as Sales ,CASE 
WHEN  pay_type = 1  THEN  sum(tot_net) 
WHEN  pay_type = 2 THEN   sum(visa) 
WHEN  pay_type = 4 THEN   sum(visa + cash)
WHEN  pay_type = -1 THEN  sum(tot_net) 
End as  ReturnSales
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (inv_type=2 or inv_type=3) and proc_type=2 and (@StartDate is null or date&gt;=@StartDate)and (@EndDate is null or date&lt;=@EndDate)group by pay_type   ) as tb group by PayTypeID
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[MonthlySales]    Script Date: 10/2/2022 2:35:56 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MonthlySales]
	@branch  [int] ,
	@inv_type  [int] ,
	@pay_type  [int] ,
	@Seller  [int] 
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
select InvDate,InvYear,InvMonth , sum(InvCount) as InvCount,sum(InvReturnCount) as InvReturnCount ,sum(InvTotal)as InvTotal , sum(ReturnTotal) as ReturnTotal, sum(InvTotal-ReturnTotal) as SaleNet from
(select  CONCAT(CAST(MONTH(date) AS VARCHAR(2)) , '-' , CAST(YEAR(date) AS VARCHAR(4))) as InvDate,0 as InvCount, sum(InvTotal) as InvTotal ,0 as InvReturnCount,0 as ReturnTotal,YEAR(date) as InvYear, month(date) as InvMonth
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (@inv_type is null or(inv_type=@inv_type or inv_type=@inv_type)) and proc_type=1 and IS_Deleted=0  group by year(date), month(date)
union all
select  CONCAT(CAST(MONTH(date) AS VARCHAR(2)) , '-' , CAST(YEAR(date) AS VARCHAR(4))) as InvDate ,0 as InvCount,0 as InvTotal, count(*) as InvReturnCount, sum(InvTotal) as ReturnTotal ,YEAR(date) as InvYear, month(date) as InvMonth
from inv where (@branch is null or branch =@branch ) and (@inv_type is null or(inv_type=@inv_type or inv_type=@inv_type)) and proc_type=2 and IS_Deleted=0 group by year(date), month(date)) as tb group by InvDate,InvYear,InvMonth order by InvYear,InvMonth
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[CalcItemStock]    Script Date: 06-Mar-23 3:42:19 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CalcProductStocks]
	@branch [int],
	@Inventory [int],
	@item [int],
	@stock Float out
WITH EXECUTE AS CALLER
AS
BEGIN
 set @stock = (select Quantity from ProductStocks where ProductId=@item and BranchId=@branch and UnitId=(select unit from Items where id=@item))   
	if (@stock is null) set @stock=0
END
GO

/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[funCalculateSerialNoSummary]    Script Date: 10/8/2023 1:33:37 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[funCalculateSerialNoSummary](
    @ItemSerialNo NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @itemid INT = NULL,
	@Branch INT = NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
    SELECT
        itemid,
        ItemSerialNo,
        SUM(stockIn - stockOut) AS result
    FROM
    (
        SELECT
            itemid,
            ItemSerialNo,
            SUM(ItemQuantity) AS stockIn,
            0 AS stockOut
        FROM
            InvoiceItemDetail
        LEFT JOIN
            Inv ON InvoiceItemDetail.InvGlobalID = Inv.InvGlobalID
        WHERE
			 InvoiceItemDetail.InvertoryImpact=1 
            AND Inv.IS_Deleted = 0
            AND (@ItemSerialNo IS NULL OR ItemSerialNo = @ItemSerialNo)
            AND (@itemid IS NULL OR itemid = @itemid)
        GROUP BY
            itemid,
            ItemSerialNo

        UNION ALL

        SELECT
            itemid,
            ItemSerialNo,
            0 AS stockIn,
            SUM(ItemQuantity) AS stockOut
        FROM
            InvoiceItemDetail
        LEFT JOIN
            Inv ON InvoiceItemDetail.InvGlobalID = Inv.InvGlobalID
        WHERE
            Inv.IS_Deleted = 0
			AND InvoiceItemDetail.InvertoryImpact=2
            AND (@ItemSerialNo IS NULL OR ItemSerialNo = @ItemSerialNo)
            AND (@itemid IS NULL OR itemid = @itemid)
			AND (@Branch IS NULL OR inv.branch = @Branch)
        GROUP BY
            itemid,
            ItemSerialNo
    ) AS tb
    GROUP BY
        itemid,
        ItemSerialNo
);
GO
/****** Object:  UserDefinedFunction [dbo].[funGetInvoiceItemDetails]    Script Date: 10/8/2023 1:34:59 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[funGetInvoiceItemDetails]
    (@ItemSerialNo NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Branch INT = NULL,
	@ItemId INT = NULL,
	@StartDate DATE = NULL,
    @EndDate DATE = NULL)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
    SELECT
        InvoiceItemDetail.ItemId,
        InvoiceItemDetail.ItemSerialNo,
		InvoiceItemDetail.InvertoryImpact,
		InvoiceItemDetail.InvGlobalID,
        inv.inv_type,
		inv.proc_type,
		inv.date as InvDate,
		inv.id as InvNo,
        items.name AS ItemName,
        items.code AS ItemCode,
		Branches.name as BranchName
    FROM
        InvoiceItemDetail
    LEFT JOIN
        Inv ON InvoiceItemDetail.InvGlobalID = inv.InvGlobalID
    LEFT JOIN
        Items ON InvoiceItemDetail.ItemId = items.id
	LEFT JOIN
	   Branches ON inv.branch = Branches.branchID
    WHERE
        (@ItemSerialNo IS NULL OR ItemSerialNo = @ItemSerialNo)
        AND (@Branch IS NULL OR inv.branch = @Branch)
		AND (@ItemId IS NULL OR InvoiceItemDetail.ItemId=@ItemId)
        AND inv.IS_Deleted = 0
		AND inv.proc_type &lt;&gt; 4
		AND (
            (@StartDate IS NULL AND @EndDate IS NULL)
            OR (inv.date BETWEEN  @StartDate AND @EndDate )
        )
	
);
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[proGetCategorySaleByDay]    Script Date: 11/5/2023 4:59:39 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[proGetCategorySaleByDay]
    @branch INT,
    @inv_type INT,
    @StartDate DATE,
    @EndDate DATE,
	@CategoryID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT 
        ItemsCategory.name AS CategoryName,
        ItemsCategory.Code AS CtgyCode,
        ItemsCategory.CategoryID,
        SUM(Sales) AS Sales,
        SUM(ReturnSales) AS ReturnSales,
        SUM(Sales - ReturnSales) - SUM(InvoiceDiscount1 - InvoiceDiscount2) AS SaleNet,
        InvDate
    FROM
    (
        SELECT
            ItemId ,  
			SUM((val1 * exchange_price) - discount) as Sales 
			,0 as ReturnSales,
			SUM(round((exchange_price * minus  /InvSum),2)) InvoiceDiscount1,
			0 InvoiceDiscount2,
			CAST(inv.date AS DATE) as InvDate
        FROM Inv_Sub
        LEFT JOIN inv ON Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID = Inv.InvGlobalID
        WHERE
             Inv.IS_Deleted = 0
            AND (@branch IS NULL OR branch = @branch)
            AND inv_type = @inv_type
			AND  inv.proc_type=1
            AND (@StartDate IS NULL OR date &gt;= @StartDate)
            AND (@EndDate IS NULL OR date &lt;= @EndDate)
        GROUP BY ItemId, inv.date
        UNION ALL
        SELECT
           ItemId , 
		   0 as Sales ,
		   SUM((val1 * exchange_price) - discount) as ReturnSales,
		   0 InvoiceDiscount1,
		   SUM(round((exchange_price * minus  /InvSum),2)) InvoiceDiscount2,
		   CAST(inv.date AS DATE) as InvDate
         FROM Inv_Sub
        LEFT JOIN inv ON Inv_Sub.InvGlobalID = Inv.InvGlobalID
        WHERE
             Inv.IS_Deleted = 0
            AND (@branch IS NULL OR branch = @branch)
            AND inv_type = @inv_type
			AND  inv.proc_type=2
            AND (@StartDate IS NULL OR date &gt;= @StartDate)
            AND (@EndDate IS NULL OR date &lt;= @EndDate)
        GROUP BY ItemId, inv.date
    ) AS tb
	
    LEFT JOIN Items ON Items.id = tb.ItemId
    LEFT JOIN ItemsCategory ON Items.group_id = ItemsCategory.CategoryId
	where  (@CategoryID IS NULL OR ItemsCategory.CategoryID = @CategoryID)
    GROUP BY CategoryID, ItemsCategory.name, ItemsCategory.Code, tb.InvDate
    ORDER BY InvDate DESC;
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[GetAccountStatement]    Script Date: 8/4/2024 1:26:33 PM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetAccountStatement]
    @ParentCode NVARCHAR(50),
    @StartDate DATETIME,
    @EndDate DATETIME,
    @Branch NVARCHAR(50),
    @ShowPreviousBalance BIT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- CTE to get account hierarchy
    WITH AccountHierarchy AS (
        SELECT 
            Code,
            AName,
            Type,
            ParentCode
        FROM 
            Accounts_Index
        WHERE 
            Code = @ParentCode
        
        UNION ALL
        
        SELECT 
            a.Code,
            a.AName,
            a.Type,
            a.ParentCode
        FROM 
            Accounts_Index a
        INNER JOIN 
            AccountHierarchy ah ON a.ParentCode = ah.Code
    ),

    -- CTE to calculate previous balance for child accounts
    PreviousBalance AS (
        SELECT 
            @ParentCode AS Code,
             SUM(es.dept) as PreviousDept,
			 SUM(es.credit) AS PreviousCredit
        FROM 
            entry e
        INNER JOIN 
            entry_sub es ON e.GlobalID = es.EntryGlobalID
        WHERE 
            es.acc_no IN (SELECT Code FROM AccountHierarchy WHERE Type = 2)
            AND e.is_Deleted = 0 -- Exclude deleted entries if needed
            AND e.date &lt; @StartDate
            AND (@Branch IS NULL OR e.branch = @Branch)
    ),

    -- CTE to get the main statement
    MainStatement AS (
        SELECT 
            ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY e.date) AS DgvRank,
            es.acc_no AS DgvAccCode, 
            ah.AName AS DgvAccName,
            e.doc_No AS DgvEntryNo, 
            es.dept AS DgvDept, 
            es.credit AS DgvCredit,
            e.date AS DgvDate,
            es.notes AS DgvNote,
            es.EntryGlobalID AS DgvGenralNo,
            b.name AS DgvBranch,
			0 as DgvBalance,
            '' DgvBalanceStatus,
            et.Name AS DgvEntryType
        FROM 
            entry e
        INNER JOIN 
            entry_sub es ON e.GlobalID = es.EntryGlobalID
        INNER JOIN 
            AccountHierarchy ah ON es.acc_no = ah.Code
		LEFT JOIN 
            Branches b ON e.branch = b.BranchId
			LEFT JOIN
			EntryTypes et on e.type=et.Id 
        WHERE 
            es.acc_no IN (SELECT Code FROM AccountHierarchy)
            AND e.is_Deleted = 0 -- Exclude deleted entries if needed
            AND (@StartDate IS NULL OR e.date &gt;= @StartDate)
            AND (@EndDate IS NULL OR e.date &lt;= @EndDate)
            AND (@Branch IS NULL OR e.branch = @Branch)
    )

    -- Combine the previous balance with the main statement based on @ShowPreviousBalance
    SELECT
        DgvRank,
        DgvAccCode,
        DgvAccName,
        DgvEntryNo,
        DgvDept,
        DgvCredit,
        DgvDate,
        DgvNote,
        DgvGenralNo,
        DgvBranch,
        DgvBalance,
        DgvBalanceStatus,
        DgvEntryType
    FROM (
        -- Previous balance row
        SELECT
            0 AS DgvRank, -- Use -1 to ensure it appears first
            pb.Code AS DgvAccCode,
            ai.AName AS DgvAccName,
            NULL AS DgvEntryNo,
            pb.PreviousDept AS DgvDept,
            pb.PreviousCredit AS DgvCredit,
            @StartDate AS DgvDate,
            'Previous Balance' AS DgvNote,
            NULL AS DgvGenralNo,
            NULL AS DgvBranch,
            0 AS DgvBalance,
		  'Previous Balance' DgvBalanceStatus,
            'Previous Balance' AS DgvEntryType
        FROM
            PreviousBalance pb
        INNER JOIN
            Accounts_Index ai ON pb.Code = ai.Code
        WHERE @ShowPreviousBalance = 1 

        UNION ALL

        -- Main statement rows
        SELECT
            ms.DgvRank,
            ms.DgvAccCode,
            ms.DgvAccName,
            ms.DgvEntryNo,
            ms.DgvDept,
            ms.DgvCredit,
            ms.DgvDate,
            ms.DgvNote,
            ms.DgvGenralNo,
            ms.DgvBranch,
            ms.DgvBalance,
            ms.DgvBalanceStatus,
            ms.DgvEntryType
        FROM
            MainStatement ms
    ) AS CombinedResult
    ORDER BY
        DgvRank, DgvDate;
END;
GO
